{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeD3XViR8Ej6MHrLycgQo","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Foundry and Node are both available. Here's my plan:\n\n- **Contracts (Foundry):** A `TipJar` contract that takes the USDC token address in its constructor (real Base USDC in prod, a local mock in dev), records tips as events + storage for the feed, and lets the owner withdraw. A `MockUSDC` (6-decimal ERC20 with mint) so local wallets actually have USDC to tip. Deploy script + tests.\n- **Frontend (Vite + React + wagmi/viem):** connect-wallet flow, live tip feed, and a tip form (approve → tip).\n- **README** with the full local run recipe. No public deployment — only local anvil.\n\nLet me scaffold the Foundry project."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1555,"cache_read_input_tokens":15504,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1555},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1f29d859-7c73-4144-867e-a953c615fdc4","uuid":"802886d8-7222-447c-a346-d2cb3b13cb95","timestamp":"2026-08-20T00:21:39.726Z","request_id":"req_011CeD3XUyWt3LmnBuBrkcho"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeD3gQ5auMEDok9LjSXZf","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Now the TipJar contract:"}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":416,"cache_read_input_tokens":21036,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":416},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1f29d859-7c73-4144-867e-a953c615fdc4","uuid":"533ab36c-b163-4aab-8d0a-7fb48c5c08ff","timestamp":"2026-08-20T00:23:15.036Z","request_id":"req_011CeD3gPKSxmEipfjQAkbVk"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeD3gQ5auMEDok9LjSXZf","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_017MyknBYGvhJGBuqe8by1xB","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-20T002040Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/contracts/src/TipJar.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity 0.8.24;\n\nimport {IERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol\";\nimport {SafeERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol\";\n\n/// @title TipJar\n/// @notice A jar that collects USDC tips. Each tip is stored on-chain (so a feed\n///         can be rebuilt from state) and emitted as an event (so a feed can be\n///         streamed cheaply). The token address is injected in the constructor so\n///         the same contract works against real Base USDC in production and a mock\n///         USDC on a local chain.\ncontract TipJar {\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n\n    struct Tip {\n        address from; // tipper\n        uint128 amount; // USDC amount, 6 decimals (fits comfortably in uint128)\n        uint64 timestamp; // block time of the tip\n        string message; // optional note left with the tip\n    }\n\n    /// @notice The ERC20 token accepted as tips (USDC).\n    IERC20 public immutable token;\n\n    /// @notice The account allowed to withdraw collected tips.\n    address public owner;\n\n    /// @notice Append-only list of every tip, forming the feed.\n    Tip[] private _tips;\n\n    /// @notice Running total of every tip ever received.\n    uint256 public totalTipped;\n\n    event Tipped(\n        uint256 indexed index,\n        address indexed from,\n        uint256 amount,\n        string message,\n        uint256 timestamp\n    );\n    event Withdrawn(address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n    event OwnershipTransferred(address indexed previousOwner, address indexed newOwner);\n\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error NotOwner();\n    error NothingToWithdraw();\n    error ZeroAddress();\n\n    modifier onlyOwner() {\n        if (msg.sender != owner) revert NotOwner();\n        _;\n    }\n\n    /// @param _token Address of the USDC token this jar accepts.\n    constructor(address _token) {\n        if (_token == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        token = IERC20(_token);\n        owner = msg.sender;\n        emit OwnershipTransferred(address(0), msg.sender);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Send a USDC tip. The caller must have approved this contract for\n    ///         at least `amount` of USDC beforehand.\n    /// @param amount USDC amount (6 decimals).\n    /// @param message Optional public note to attach to the tip.\n    function tip(uint256 amount, string calldata message) external {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        // Pull the tokens in. SafeERC20 reverts on any failure.\n        token.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n\n        uint256 index = _tips.length;\n        _tips.push(\n            Tip({\n                from: msg.sender,\n                amount: uint128(amount),\n                timestamp: uint64(block.timestamp),\n                message: message\n            })\n        );\n        totalTipped += amount;\n\n        emit Tipped(index, msg.sender, amount, message, block.timestamp);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Number of tips recorded so far.\n    function tipCount() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return _tips.length;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Read a single tip by index.\n    function getTip(uint256 index) external view returns (Tip memory) {\n        return _tips[index];\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Read the most recent `count` tips, newest first.\n    /// @dev Convenience getter so the UI can render a feed in one call.\n    function getLatestTips(uint256 count) external view returns (Tip[] memory page) {\n        uint256 total = _tips.length;\n        if (count > total) count = total;\n        page = new Tip[](count);\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i < count; i++) {\n            page[i] = _tips[total - 1 - i];\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Current USDC balance held by the jar (i.e. withdrawable amount).\n    function balance() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return token.balanceOf(address(this));\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Withdraw all collected USDC to the owner.\n    function withdraw() external onlyOwner {\n        uint256 amount = token.balanceOf(address(this));\n        if (amount == 0) revert NothingToWithdraw();\n        token.safeTransfer(owner, amount);\n        emit Withdrawn(owner, amount);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Hand the jar (and its withdraw rights) to a new owner.\n    function transferOwnership(address newOwner) external onlyOwner {\n        if (newOwner == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        emit OwnershipTransferred(owner, newOwner);\n        owner = newOwner;\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":416,"cache_read_input_tokens":21036,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":416},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1f29d859-7c73-4144-867e-a953c615fdc4","uuid":"ac15d077-5770-42c6-9154-6ebae47655fb","timestamp":"2026-08-20T00:23:32.443Z","request_id":"req_011CeD3gPKSxmEipfjQAkbVk"}

Each tip is stored on-chain (so a feed\n///         can be rebuilt from state) and emitted as an event (so a feed can be\n///         streamed cheaply). The token address is injected in the constructor so\n///         the same contract works against real Base USDC in production and a mock\n///         USDC on a local chain.\ncontract TipJar {\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n\n    struct Tip {\n        address from; // tipper\n        uint128 amount; // USDC amount, 6 decimals (fits comfortably in uint128)\n        uint64 timestamp; // block time of the tip\n        string message; // optional note left with the tip\n    }\n\n    /// @notice The ERC20 token accepted as tips (USDC).\n    IERC20 public immutable token;\n\n    /// @notice The account allowed to withdraw collected tips.\n    address public owner;\n\n    /// @notice Append-only list of every tip, forming the feed.\n    Tip[] private _tips;\n\n    /// @notice Running total of every tip ever received.\n    uint256 public totalTipped;\n\n    event Tipped(\n        uint256 indexed index,\n        address indexed from,\n        uint256 amount,\n        string message,\n        uint256 timestamp\n    );\n    event Withdrawn(address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n    event OwnershipTransferred(address indexed previousOwner, address indexed newOwner);\n\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error NotOwner();\n    error NothingToWithdraw();\n    error ZeroAddress();\n\n    modifier onlyOwner() {\n        if (msg.sender != owner) revert NotOwner();\n        _;\n    }\n\n    /// @param _token Address of the USDC token this jar accepts.\n    constructor(address _token) {\n        if (_token == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        token = IERC20(_token);\n        owner = msg.sender;\n        emit OwnershipTransferred(address(0), msg.sender);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Send a USDC tip. The caller must have approved this contract for\n    ///         at least `amount` of USDC beforehand.\n    /// @param amount USDC amount (6 decimals).\n    /// @param message Optional public note to attach to the tip.\n    function tip(uint256 amount, string calldata message) external {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        // Pull the tokens in. SafeERC20 reverts on any failure.\n        token.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n\n        uint256 index = _tips.length;\n        _tips.push(\n            Tip({\n                from: msg.sender,\n                amount: uint128(amount),\n                timestamp: uint64(block.timestamp),\n                message: message\n            })\n        );\n        totalTipped += amount;\n\n        emit Tipped(index, msg.sender, amount, message, block.timestamp);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Number of tips recorded so far.\n    function tipCount() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return _tips.length;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Read a single tip by index.\n    function getTip(uint256 index) external view returns (Tip memory) {\n        return _tips[index];\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Read the most recent `count` tips, newest first.\n    /// @dev Convenience getter so the UI can render a feed in one call.\n    function getLatestTips(uint256 count) external view returns (Tip[] memory page) {\n        uint256 total = _tips.length;\n        if (count > total) count = total;\n        page = new Tip[](count);\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i < count; i++) {\n            page[i] = _tips[total - 1 - i];\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Current USDC balance held by the jar (i.e. withdrawable amount).\n    function balance() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return token.balanceOf(address(this));\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Withdraw all collected USDC to the owner.\n    function withdraw() external onlyOwner {\n        uint256 amount = token.balanceOf(address(this));\n        if (amount == 0) revert NothingToWithdraw();\n        token.safeTransfer(owner, amount);\n        emit Withdrawn(owner, amount);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Hand the jar (and its withdraw rights) to a new owner.\n    function transferOwnership(address newOwner) external onlyOwner {\n        if (newOwner == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        emit OwnershipTransferred(owner, newOwner);\n        owner = newOwner;\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

Anyone can mint to themselves\n///         via the faucet so local wallets have something to tip with. This is a\n///         test helper only and must never be deployed to a public network.\ncontract MockUSDC is ERC20 {\n    constructor() ERC20(\"Mock USD Coin\", \"USDC\") {}\n\n    /// @dev USDC uses 6 decimals, not the ERC20 default of 18.\n    function decimals() public pure override returns (uint8) {\n        return 6;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Mint USDC to any address (local faucet).\n    function mint(address to, uint256 amount) external {\n        _mint(to, amount);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Mint 1,000 USDC to the caller.\n    function faucet() external {\n        _mint(msg.sender, 1_000 * 10 ** 6);\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1945,"cache_read_input_tokens":21452,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1945},"output_tokens":20,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1f29d859-7c73-4144-867e-a953c615fdc4","uuid":"140e9661-8207-4abd-983f-bd76020d56d9","timestamp":"2026-08-20T00:23:39.741Z","request_id":"req_011CeD3hoLqtHqfsav9X2R7Q"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeD3iMEjtCB3qHSUntKFd","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01FcapZU1DRa6MqQmsLuWLC2","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-20T002040Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/contracts/test/TipJar.t.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity 0.8.24;\n\nimport {Test} from \"forge-std/Test.sol\";\nimport {TipJar} from \"../src/TipJar.sol\";\nimport {MockUSDC} from \"../src/MockUSDC.sol\";\n\ncontract TipJarTest is Test {\n    MockUSDC internal usdc;\n    TipJar internal jar;\n\n    address internal owner = makeAddr(\"owner\");\n    address internal alice = makeAddr(\"alice\");\n    address internal bob = makeAddr(\"bob\");\n\n    uint256 internal constant ONE_USDC = 1e6;\n\n    event Tipped(\n        uint256 indexed index,\n        address indexed from,\n        uint256 amount,\n        string message,\n        uint256 timestamp\n    );\n\n    function setUp() public {\n        usdc = new MockUSDC();\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        jar = new TipJar(address(usdc));\n\n        usdc.mint(alice, 100 * ONE_USDC);\n        usdc.mint(bob, 100 * ONE_USDC);\n    }\n\n    function _tip(address who, uint256 amount, string memory message) internal {\n        vm.startPrank(who);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), amount);\n        jar.tip(amount, message);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_ConstructorSetsState() public view {\n        assertEq(address(jar.token()), address(usdc));\n        assertEq(jar.owner(), owner);\n        assertEq(jar.tipCount(), 0);\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), 0);\n    }\n\n    function test_ConstructorRejectsZeroToken() public {\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.ZeroAddress.selector);\n        new TipJar(address(0));\n    }\n\n    function test_TipTransfersAndRecords() public {\n        vm.expectEmit(true, true, false, true, address(jar));\n        emit Tipped(0, alice, 5 * ONE_USDC, \"gm\", block.timestamp);\n        _tip(alice, 5 * ONE_USDC, \"gm\");\n\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(address(jar)), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(alice), 95 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.tipCount(), 1);\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.balance(), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n\n        TipJar.Tip memory t = jar.getTip(0);\n        assertEq(t.from, alice);\n        assertEq(t.amount, uint128(5 * ONE_USDC));\n        assertEq(t.message, \"gm\");\n        assertEq(t.timestamp, uint64(block.timestamp));\n    }\n\n    function test_TipRevertsOnZeroAmount() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.ZeroAmount.selector);\n        jar.tip(0, \"nope\");\n    }\n\n    function test_TipRevertsWithoutApproval() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(); // SafeERC20 / allowance failure\n        jar.tip(ONE_USDC, \"no approval\");\n    }\n\n    function test_GetLatestTipsNewestFirst() public {\n        _tip(alice, ONE_USDC, \"one\");\n        _tip(bob, 2 * ONE_USDC, \"two\");\n        _tip(alice, 3 * ONE_USDC, \"three\");\n\n        TipJar.Tip[] memory latest = jar.getLatestTips(2);\n        assertEq(latest.length, 2);\n        assertEq(latest[0].message, \"three\");\n        assertEq(latest[1].message, \"two\");\n    }\n\n    function test_GetLatestTipsClampsCount() public {\n        _tip(alice, ONE_USDC, \"one\");\n        TipJar.Tip[] memory latest = jar.getLatestTips(50);\n        assertEq(latest.length, 1);\n        assertEq(latest[0].message, \"one\");\n    }\n\n    function test_WithdrawSendsToOwner() public {\n        _tip(alice, 10 * ONE_USDC, \"thanks\");\n\n        uint256 before = usdc.balanceOf(owner);\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        jar.withdraw();\n\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(owner), before + 10 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.balance(), 0);\n        // totalTipped is cumulative and must not reset on withdraw.\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), 10 * ONE_USDC);\n    }\n\n    function test_WithdrawOnlyOwner() public {\n        _tip(alice, ONE_USDC, \"x\");\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.NotOwner.selector);\n        jar.withdraw();\n    }\n\n    function test_WithdrawRevertsWhenEmpty() public {\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.NothingToWithdraw.selector);\n        jar.withdraw();\n    }\n\n    function test_TransferOwnership() public {\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        jar.transferOwnership(alice);\n        assertEq(jar.owner(), alice);\n\n        _tip(bob, ONE_USDC, \"y\");\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        jar.withdraw();\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(alice), 100 * ONE_USDC - ONE_USDC + ONE_USDC);\n    }\n\n    function test_TransferOwnershipOnlyOwner() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.NotOwner.selector);\n        jar.transferOwnership(bob);\n    }\n\n    function testFuzz_MultipleTipsAccumulate(uint96 a, uint96 b) public {\n        vm.assume(a > 0 && b > 0);\n        usdc.mint(alice, uint256(a) + uint256(b));\n\n        _tip(alice, a, \"a\");\n        _tip(alice, b, \"b\");\n\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), uint256(a) + uint256(b));\n        assertEq(jar.tipCount(), 2);\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":691,"cache_read_input_tokens":23397,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":691},"output_tokens":6,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1f29d859-7c73-4144-867e-a953c615fdc4","uuid":"fd7912e9-6c8c-4418-ac6b-8dcd021315f5","timestamp":"2026-08-20T00:24:01.967Z","request_id":"req_011CeD3iLRsJPkKZQi7Y3DMH"}

ONE_USDC, \"y\");\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        jar.withdraw();\n        // alice keeps her original 100 USDC and receives the 1 USDC bob tipped.\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(alice), 101 * ONE_USDC);","originalFile":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity 0.8.24;\n\nimport {Test} from \"forge-std/Test.sol\";\nimport {TipJar} from \"../src/TipJar.sol\";\nimport {MockUSDC} from \"../src/MockUSDC.sol\";\n\ncontract TipJarTest is Test {\n    MockUSDC internal usdc;\n    TipJar internal jar;\n\n    address internal owner = makeAddr(\"owner\");\n    address internal alice = makeAddr(\"alice\");\n    address internal bob = makeAddr(\"bob\");\n\n    uint256 internal constant ONE_USDC = 1e6;\n\n    event Tipped(\n        uint256 indexed index,\n        address indexed from,\n        uint256 amount,\n        string message,\n        uint256 timestamp\n    );\n\n    function setUp() public {\n        usdc = new MockUSDC();\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        jar = new TipJar(address(usdc));\n\n        usdc.mint(alice, 100 * ONE_USDC);\n        usdc.mint(bob, 100 * ONE_USDC);\n    }\n\n    function _tip(address who, uint256 amount, string memory message) internal {\n        vm.startPrank(who);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), amount);\n        jar.tip(amount, message);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_ConstructorSetsState() public view {\n        assertEq(address(jar.token()), address(usdc));\n        assertEq(jar.owner(), owner);\n        assertEq(jar.tipCount(), 0);\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), 0);\n    }\n\n    function test_ConstructorRejectsZeroToken() public {\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.ZeroAddress.selector);\n        new TipJar(address(0));\n    }\n\n    function test_TipTransfersAndRecords() public {\n        vm.expectEmit(true, true, false, true, address(jar));\n        emit Tipped(0, alice, 5 * ONE_USDC, \"gm\", block.timestamp);\n        _tip(alice, 5 * ONE_USDC, \"gm\");\n\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(address(jar)), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(alice), 95 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.tipCount(), 1);\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.balance(), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n\n        TipJar.Tip memory t = jar.getTip(0);\n        assertEq(t.from, alice);\n        assertEq(t.amount, uint128(5 * ONE_USDC));\n        assertEq(t.message, \"gm\");\n        assertEq(t.timestamp, uint64(block.timestamp));\n    }\n\n    function test_TipRevertsOnZeroAmount() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.ZeroAmount.selector);\n        jar.tip(0, \"nope\");\n    }\n\n    function test_TipRevertsWithoutApproval() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(); // SafeERC20 / allowance failure\n        jar.tip(ONE_USDC, \"no approval\");\n    }\n\n    function test_GetLatestTipsNewestFirst() public {\n        _tip(alice, ONE_USDC, \"one\");\n        _tip(bob, 2 * ONE_USDC, \"two\");\n        _tip(alice, 3 * ONE_USDC, \"three\");\n\n        TipJar.Tip[] memory latest = jar.getLatestTips(2);\n        assertEq(latest.length, 2);\n        assertEq(latest[0].message, \"three\");\n        assertEq(latest[1].message, \"two\");\n    }\n\n    function test_GetLatestTipsClampsCount() public {\n        _tip(alice, ONE_USDC, \"one\");\n        TipJar.Tip[] memory latest = jar.getLatestTips(50);\n        assertEq(latest.length, 1);\n        assertEq(latest[0].message, \"one\");\n    }\n\n    function test_WithdrawSendsToOwner() public {\n        _tip(alice, 10 * ONE_USDC, \"thanks\");\n\n        uint256 before = usdc.balanceOf(owner);\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        jar.withdraw();\n\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(owner), before + 10 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.balance(), 0);\n        // totalTipped is cumulative and must not reset on withdraw.\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), 10 * ONE_USDC);\n    }\n\n    function test_WithdrawOnlyOwner() public {\n        _tip(alice, ONE_USDC, \"x\");\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.NotOwner.selector);\n        jar.withdraw();\n    }\n\n    function test_WithdrawRevertsWhenEmpty() public {\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.NothingToWithdraw.selector);\n        jar.withdraw();\n    }\n\n    function test_TransferOwnership() public {\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        jar.transferOwnership(alice);\n        assertEq(jar.owner(), alice);\n\n        _tip(bob, ONE_USDC, \"y\");\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        jar.withdraw();\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(alice), 100 * ONE_USDC - ONE_USDC + ONE_USDC);\n    }\n\n    function test_TransferOwnershipOnlyOwner() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.NotOwner.selector);\n        jar.transferOwnership(bob);\n    }\n\n    function testFuzz_MultipleTipsAccumulate(uint96 a, uint96 b) public {\n        vm.assume(a > 0 && b > 0);\n        usdc.mint(alice, uint256(a) + uint256(b));\n\n        _tip(alice, a, \"a\");\n        _tip(alice, b, \"b\");\n\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), uint256(a) + uint256(b));\n        assertEq(jar.tipCount(), 2);\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":133,"oldLines":7,"newStart":133,"newLines":8,"lines":["         _tip(bob, ONE_USDC, \"y\");","         vm.prank(alice);","         jar.withdraw();","-        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(alice), 100 * ONE_USDC - ONE_USDC + ONE_USDC);","+        // alice keeps her original 100 USDC and receives the 1 USDC bob tipped.","+        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(alice), 101 * ONE_USDC);","     }"," ","     function test_TransferOwnershipOnlyOwner() public {"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

It deploys MockUSDC + TipJar to local anvil, mints to the standard anvil accounts, and writes deployment info the frontend can consume:"}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":808,"cache_read_input_tokens":26553,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":808},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1f29d859-7c73-4144-867e-a953c615fdc4","uuid":"b116d7bc-5d1c-47b0-814e-a5c4dfb2c86d","timestamp":"2026-08-20T00:24:12.711Z","request_id":"req_011CeD3kXuVFqdZ5rocEnkPp"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeD3kZAeF8th7kB31PjoJ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_0195E9ueUcap8HUJRogh2QXX","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-20T002040Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/contracts/script/Deploy.s.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity 0.8.24;\n\nimport {Script, console2} from \"forge-std/Script.sol\";\nimport {TipJar} from \"../src/TipJar.sol\";\nimport {MockUSDC} from \"../src/MockUSDC.sol\";\n\n/// @notice Local deployment script.\n///\n/// On a local chain the real Base USDC contract does not exist, so we deploy a\n/// MockUSDC, seed the default anvil accounts with a balance, then deploy the\n/// TipJar pointing at that mock. The resulting addresses are written to\n/// `deployments/local.json` for the frontend to read.\n///\n/// To target a real network you would instead pass the canonical USDC address\n/// (Base mainnet: 0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913) to `new TipJar(...)`\n/// and skip the mock — but this project is intentionally local-only.\ncontract Deploy is Script {\n    // The first few well-known anvil dev accounts, seeded so tipping works out\n    // of the box.\n    address[] internal seedAccounts = [\n        0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266, // account #0\n        0x70997970C51812dc3A010C7d01b50e0d17dc79C8, // account #1\n        0x3C44CdDdB6a900fa2b585dd299e03d12FA4293BC // account #2\n    ];\n\n    function run() external {\n        uint256 deployerKey = vm.envUint(\"PRIVATE_KEY\");\n        address deployer = vm.addr(deployerKey);\n\n        vm.startBroadcast(deployerKey);\n\n        MockUSDC usdc = new MockUSDC();\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i < seedAccounts.length; i++) {\n            usdc.mint(seedAccounts[i], 10_000 * 10 ** 6); // 10,000 USDC each\n        }\n\n        TipJar jar = new TipJar(address(usdc));\n\n        vm.stopBroadcast();\n\n        console2.log(\"Deployer:   \", deployer);\n        console2.log(\"MockUSDC:   \", address(usdc));\n        console2.log(\"TipJar:     \", address(jar));\n\n        _writeDeployment(address(usdc), address(jar), deployer);\n    }\n\n    function _writeDeployment(address usdc, address jar, address deployer) internal {\n        string memory json = \"deployment\";\n        vm.serializeUint(json, \"chainId\", block.chainid);\n        vm.serializeAddress(json, \"usdc\", usdc);\n        vm.serializeAddress(json, \"deployer\", deployer);\n        string memory out = vm.serializeAddress(json, \"tipJar\", jar);\n\n        string memory path = \"deployments/local.json\";\n        vm.writeJson(out, path);\n        console2.log(\"Wrote deployment to\", path);\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":808,"cache_read_input_tokens":26553,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":808},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1f29d859-7c73-4144-867e-a953c615fdc4","uuid":"1101af59-6c26-4896-bac4-3403cb2a1b9d","timestamp":"2026-08-20T00:24:27.927Z","request_id":"req_011CeD3kXuVFqdZ5rocEnkPp"}

The resulting addresses are written to\n/// `deployments/local.json` for the frontend to read.\n///\n/// To target a real network you would instead pass the canonical USDC address\n/// (Base mainnet: 0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913) to `new TipJar(...)`\n/// and skip the mock — but this project is intentionally local-only.\ncontract Deploy is Script {\n    // The first few well-known anvil dev accounts, seeded so tipping works out\n    // of the box.\n    address[] internal seedAccounts = [\n        0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266, // account #0\n        0x70997970C51812dc3A010C7d01b50e0d17dc79C8, // account #1\n        0x3C44CdDdB6a900fa2b585dd299e03d12FA4293BC // account #2\n    ];\n\n    function run() external {\n        uint256 deployerKey = vm.envUint(\"PRIVATE_KEY\");\n        address deployer = vm.addr(deployerKey);\n\n        vm.startBroadcast(deployerKey);\n\n        MockUSDC usdc = new MockUSDC();\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i < seedAccounts.length; i++) {\n            usdc.mint(seedAccounts[i], 10_000 * 10 ** 6); // 10,000 USDC each\n        }\n\n        TipJar jar = new TipJar(address(usdc));\n\n        vm.stopBroadcast();\n\n        console2.log(\"Deployer:   \", deployer);\n        console2.log(\"MockUSDC:   \", address(usdc));\n        console2.log(\"TipJar:     \", address(jar));\n\n        _writeDeployment(address(usdc), address(jar), deployer);\n    }\n\n    function _writeDeployment(address usdc, address jar, address deployer) internal {\n        string memory json = \"deployment\";\n        vm.serializeUint(json, \"chainId\", block.chainid);\n        vm.serializeAddress(json, \"usdc\", usdc);\n        vm.serializeAddress(json, \"deployer\", deployer);\n        string memory out = vm.serializeAddress(json, \"tipJar\", jar);\n\n        string memory path = \"deployments/local.json\";\n        vm.writeJson(out, path);\n        console2.log(\"Wrote deployment to\", path);\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

Each tip is stored on-chain (so a feed\n///         can be rebuilt from state) and emitted as an event (so a feed can be\n///         streamed cheaply). The token address is injected in the constructor so\n///         the same contract works against real Base USDC in production and a mock\n///         USDC on a local chain.\ncontract TipJar {\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n\n    struct Tip {\n        address from; // tipper\n        uint128 amount; // USDC amount, 6 decimals (fits comfortably in uint128)\n        uint64 timestamp; // block time of the tip\n        string message; // optional note left with the tip\n    }\n\n    /// @notice The ERC20 token accepted as tips (USDC).\n    IERC20 public immutable token;\n\n    /// @notice The account allowed to withdraw collected tips.\n    address public owner;\n\n    /// @notice Append-only list of every tip, forming the feed.\n    Tip[] private _tips;\n\n    /// @notice Running total of every tip ever received.\n    uint256 public totalTipped;\n\n    event Tipped(\n        uint256 indexed index,\n        address indexed from,\n        uint256 amount,\n        string message,\n        uint256 timestamp\n    );\n    event Withdrawn(address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n    event OwnershipTransferred(address indexed previousOwner, address indexed newOwner);\n\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error NotOwner();\n    error NothingToWithdraw();\n    error ZeroAddress();\n\n    modifier onlyOwner() {\n        if (msg.sender != owner) revert NotOwner();\n        _;\n    }\n\n    /// @param _token Address of the USDC token this jar accepts.\n    constructor(address _token) {\n        if (_token == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        token = IERC20(_token);\n        owner = msg.sender;\n        emit OwnershipTransferred(address(0), msg.sender);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Send a USDC tip. The caller must have approved this contract for\n    ///         at least `amount` of USDC beforehand.\n    /// @param amount USDC amount (6 decimals).\n    /// @param message Optional public note to attach to the tip.\n    function tip(uint256 amount, string calldata message) external {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        // Pull the tokens in. SafeERC20 reverts on any failure.\n        token.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n\n        uint256 index = _tips.length;\n        _tips.push(\n            Tip({\n                from: msg.sender,\n                amount: uint128(amount),\n                timestamp: uint64(block.timestamp),\n                message: message\n            })\n        );\n        totalTipped += amount;\n\n        emit Tipped(index, msg.sender, amount, message, block.timestamp);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Number of tips recorded so far.\n    function tipCount() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return _tips.length;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Read a single tip by index.\n    function getTip(uint256 index) external view returns (Tip memory) {\n        return _tips[index];\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Read the most recent `count` tips, newest first.\n    /// @dev Convenience getter so the UI can render a feed in one call.\n    function getLatestTips(uint256 count) external view returns (Tip[] memory page) {\n        uint256 total = _tips.length;\n        if (count > total) count = total;\n        page = new Tip[](count);\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i < count; i++) {\n            page[i] = _tips[total - 1 - i];\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Current USDC balance held by the jar (i.e. withdrawable amount).\n    function balance() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return token.balanceOf(address(this));\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Withdraw all collected USDC to the owner.\n    function withdraw() external onlyOwner {\n        uint256 amount = token.balanceOf(address(this));\n        if (amount == 0) revert NothingToWithdraw();\n        token.safeTransfer(owner, amount);\n        emit Withdrawn(owner, amount);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Hand the jar (and its withdraw rights) to a new owner.\n    function transferOwnership(address newOwner) external onlyOwner {\n        if (newOwner == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        emit OwnershipTransferred(owner, newOwner);\n        owner = newOwner;\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":43,"oldLines":6,"newStart":43,"newLines":7,"lines":["     event OwnershipTransferred(address indexed previousOwner, address indexed newOwner);"," ","     error ZeroAmount();","+    error AmountTooLarge();","     error NotOwner();","     error NothingToWithdraw();","     error ZeroAddress();"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

The token address is injected in the constructor so\n///         the same contract works against real Base USDC in production and a mock\n///         USDC on a local chain.\ncontract TipJar {\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n\n    struct Tip {\n        address from; // tipper\n        uint128 amount; // USDC amount, 6 decimals (fits comfortably in uint128)\n        uint64 timestamp; // block time of the tip\n        string message; // optional note left with the tip\n    }\n\n    /// @notice The ERC20 token accepted as tips (USDC).\n    IERC20 public immutable token;\n\n    /// @notice The account allowed to withdraw collected tips.\n    address public owner;\n\n    /// @notice Append-only list of every tip, forming the feed.\n    Tip[] private _tips;\n\n    /// @notice Running total of every tip ever received.\n    uint256 public totalTipped;\n\n    event Tipped(\n        uint256 indexed index,\n        address indexed from,\n        uint256 amount,\n        string message,\n        uint256 timestamp\n    );\n    event Withdrawn(address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n    event OwnershipTransferred(address indexed previousOwner, address indexed newOwner);\n\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error AmountTooLarge();\n    error NotOwner();\n    error NothingToWithdraw();\n    error ZeroAddress();\n\n    modifier onlyOwner() {\n        if (msg.sender != owner) revert NotOwner();\n        _;\n    }\n\n    /// @param _token Address of the USDC token this jar accepts.\n    constructor(address _token) {\n        if (_token == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        token = IERC20(_token);\n        owner = msg.sender;\n        emit OwnershipTransferred(address(0), msg.sender);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Send a USDC tip. The caller must have approved this contract for\n    ///         at least `amount` of USDC beforehand.\n    /// @param amount USDC amount (6 decimals).\n    /// @param message Optional public note to attach to the tip.\n    function tip(uint256 amount, string calldata message) external {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        // Pull the tokens in. SafeERC20 reverts on any failure.\n        token.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n\n        uint256 index = _tips.length;\n        _tips.push(\n            Tip({\n                from: msg.sender,\n                amount: uint128(amount),\n                timestamp: uint64(block.timestamp),\n                message: message\n            })\n        );\n        totalTipped += amount;\n\n        emit Tipped(index, msg.sender, amount, message, block.timestamp);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Number of tips recorded so far.\n    function tipCount() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return _tips.length;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Read a single tip by index.\n    function getTip(uint256 index) external view returns (Tip memory) {\n        return _tips[index];\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Read the most recent `count` tips, newest first.\n    /// @dev Convenience getter so the UI can render a feed in one call.\n    function getLatestTips(uint256 count) external view returns (Tip[] memory page) {\n        uint256 total = _tips.length;\n        if (count > total) count = total;\n        page = new Tip[](count);\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i < count; i++) {\n            page[i] = _tips[total - 1 - i];\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Current USDC balance held by the jar (i.e. withdrawable amount).\n    function balance() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return token.balanceOf(address(this));\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Withdraw all collected USDC to the owner.\n    function withdraw() external onlyOwner {\n        uint256 amount = token.balanceOf(address(this));\n        if (amount == 0) revert NothingToWithdraw();\n        token.safeTransfer(owner, amount);\n        emit Withdrawn(owner, amount);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Hand the jar (and its withdraw rights) to a new owner.\n    function transferOwnership(address newOwner) external onlyOwner {\n        if (newOwner == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        emit OwnershipTransferred(owner, newOwner);\n        owner = newOwner;\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":67,"oldLines":6,"newStart":67,"newLines":9,"lines":["     /// @param message Optional public note to attach to the tip.","     function tip(uint256 amount, string calldata message) external {","         if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();","+        // Tips are stored as uint128; Each tip is stored on-chain (so a feed\n///         can be rebuilt from state) and emitted as an event (so a feed can be\n///         streamed cheaply). The token address is injected in the constructor so\n///         the same contract works against real Base USDC in production and a mock\n///         USDC on a local chain.\ncontract TipJar {\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n\n    struct Tip {\n        address from; // tipper\n        uint128 amount; // USDC amount, 6 decimals (fits comfortably in uint128)\n        uint64 timestamp; // block time of the tip\n        string message; // optional note left with the tip\n    }\n\n    /// @notice The ERC20 token accepted as tips (USDC).\n    IERC20 public immutable token;\n\n    /// @notice The account allowed to withdraw collected tips.\n    address public owner;\n\n    /// @notice Append-only list of every tip, forming the feed.\n    Tip[] private _tips;\n\n    /// @notice Running total of every tip ever received.\n    uint256 public totalTipped;\n\n    event Tipped(\n        uint256 indexed index,\n        address indexed from,\n        uint256 amount,\n        string message,\n        uint256 timestamp\n    );\n    event Withdrawn(address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n    event OwnershipTransferred(address indexed previousOwner, address indexed newOwner);\n\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error AmountTooLarge();\n    error NotOwner();\n    error NothingToWithdraw();\n    error ZeroAddress();\n\n    modifier onlyOwner() {\n        if (msg.sender != owner) revert NotOwner();\n        _;\n    }\n\n    /// @param _token Address of the USDC token this jar accepts.\n    constructor(address _token) {\n        if (_token == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        token = IERC20(_token);\n        owner = msg.sender;\n        emit OwnershipTransferred(address(0), msg.sender);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Send a USDC tip. The caller must have approved this contract for\n    ///         at least `amount` of USDC beforehand.\n    /// @param amount USDC amount (6 decimals).\n    /// @param message Optional public note to attach to the tip.\n    function tip(uint256 amount, string calldata message) external {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        // Tips are stored as uint128; guard the cast rather than truncate silently.\n        // (uint128 max is ~3.4e38, i.e. ~3.4e32 USDC — far beyond any real tip.)\n        if (amount > type(uint128).max) revert AmountTooLarge();\n\n        // Pull the tokens in. SafeERC20 reverts on any failure.\n        token.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n\n        uint256 index = _tips.length;\n        _tips.push(\n            Tip({\n                from: msg.sender,\n                amount: uint128(amount),\n                timestamp: uint64(block.timestamp),\n                message: message\n            })\n        );\n        totalTipped += amount;\n\n        emit Tipped(index, msg.sender, amount, message, block.timestamp);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Number of tips recorded so far.\n    function tipCount() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return _tips.length;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Read a single tip by index.\n    function getTip(uint256 index) external view returns (Tip memory) {\n        return _tips[index];\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Read the most recent `count` tips, newest first.\n    /// @dev Convenience getter so the UI can render a feed in one call.\n    function getLatestTips(uint256 count) external view returns (Tip[] memory page) {\n        uint256 total = _tips.length;\n        if (count > total) count = total;\n        page = new Tip[](count);\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i < count; i++) {\n            page[i] = _tips[total - 1 - i];\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Current USDC balance held by the jar (i.e. withdrawable amount).\n    function balance() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return token.balanceOf(address(this));\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Withdraw all collected USDC to the owner.\n    function withdraw() external onlyOwner {\n        uint256 amount = token.balanceOf(address(this));\n        if (amount == 0) revert NothingToWithdraw();\n        token.safeTransfer(owner, amount);\n        emit Withdrawn(owner, amount);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Hand the jar (and its withdraw rights) to a new owner.\n    function transferOwnership(address newOwner) external onlyOwner {\n        if (newOwner == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        emit OwnershipTransferred(owner, newOwner);\n        owner = newOwner;\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":78,"oldLines":7,"newStart":78,"newLines":7,"lines":["         _tips.push(","             Tip({","                 from: msg.sender,","-                amount: uint128(amount),","+                amount: uint128(amount), // forge-lint: disable-next-line(unsafe-typecast)","                 timestamp: uint64(block.timestamp),","                 message: message","             })"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

vm.expectEmit(true, true, false, true, address(jar));\n        emit Tipped(0, alice, 5 * ONE_USDC, \"gm\", block.timestamp);\n        jar.tip(5 * ONE_USDC, \"gm\");\n        vm.stopPrank();","originalFile":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity 0.8.24;\n\nimport {Test} from \"forge-std/Test.sol\";\nimport {TipJar} from \"../src/TipJar.sol\";\nimport {MockUSDC} from \"../src/MockUSDC.sol\";\n\ncontract TipJarTest is Test {\n    MockUSDC internal usdc;\n    TipJar internal jar;\n\n    address internal owner = makeAddr(\"owner\");\n    address internal alice = makeAddr(\"alice\");\n    address internal bob = makeAddr(\"bob\");\n\n    uint256 internal constant ONE_USDC = 1e6;\n\n    event Tipped(\n        uint256 indexed index,\n        address indexed from,\n        uint256 amount,\n        string message,\n        uint256 timestamp\n    );\n\n    function setUp() public {\n        usdc = new MockUSDC();\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        jar = new TipJar(address(usdc));\n\n        usdc.mint(alice, 100 * ONE_USDC);\n        usdc.mint(bob, 100 * ONE_USDC);\n    }\n\n    function _tip(address who, uint256 amount, string memory message) internal {\n        vm.startPrank(who);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), amount);\n        jar.tip(amount, message);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_ConstructorSetsState() public view {\n        assertEq(address(jar.token()), address(usdc));\n        assertEq(jar.owner(), owner);\n        assertEq(jar.tipCount(), 0);\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), 0);\n    }\n\n    function test_ConstructorRejectsZeroToken() public {\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.ZeroAddress.selector);\n        new TipJar(address(0));\n    }\n\n    function test_TipTransfersAndRecords() public {\n        vm.expectEmit(true, true, false, true, address(jar));\n        emit Tipped(0, alice, 5 * ONE_USDC, \"gm\", block.timestamp);\n        _tip(alice, 5 * ONE_USDC, \"gm\");\n\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(address(jar)), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(alice), 95 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.tipCount(), 1);\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.balance(), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n\n        TipJar.Tip memory t = jar.getTip(0);\n        assertEq(t.from, alice);\n        assertEq(t.amount, uint128(5 * ONE_USDC));\n        assertEq(t.message, \"gm\");\n        assertEq(t.timestamp, uint64(block.timestamp));\n    }\n\n    function test_TipRevertsOnZeroAmount() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.ZeroAmount.selector);\n        jar.tip(0, \"nope\");\n    }\n\n    function test_TipRevertsWithoutApproval() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(); // SafeERC20 / allowance failure\n        jar.tip(ONE_USDC, \"no approval\");\n    }\n\n    function test_GetLatestTipsNewestFirst() public {\n        _tip(alice, ONE_USDC, \"one\");\n        _tip(bob, 2 * ONE_USDC, \"two\");\n        _tip(alice, 3 * ONE_USDC, \"three\");\n\n        TipJar.Tip[] memory latest = jar.getLatestTips(2);\n        assertEq(latest.length, 2);\n        assertEq(latest[0].message, \"three\");\n        assertEq(latest[1].message, \"two\");\n    }\n\n    function test_GetLatestTipsClampsCount() public {\n        _tip(alice, ONE_USDC, \"one\");\n        TipJar.Tip[] memory latest = jar.getLatestTips(50);\n        assertEq(latest.length, 1);\n        assertEq(latest[0].message, \"one\");\n    }\n\n    function test_WithdrawSendsToOwner() public {\n        _tip(alice, 10 * ONE_USDC, \"thanks\");\n\n        uint256 before = usdc.balanceOf(owner);\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        jar.withdraw();\n\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(owner), before + 10 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.balance(), 0);\n        // totalTipped is cumulative and must not reset on withdraw.\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), 10 * ONE_USDC);\n    }\n\n    function test_WithdrawOnlyOwner() public {\n        _tip(alice, ONE_USDC, \"x\");\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.NotOwner.selector);\n        jar.withdraw();\n    }\n\n    function test_WithdrawRevertsWhenEmpty() public {\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.NothingToWithdraw.selector);\n        jar.withdraw();\n    }\n\n    function test_TransferOwnership() public {\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        jar.transferOwnership(alice);\n        assertEq(jar.owner(), alice);\n\n        _tip(bob, ONE_USDC, \"y\");\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        jar.withdraw();\n        // alice keeps her original 100 USDC and receives the 1 USDC bob tipped.\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(alice), 101 * ONE_USDC);\n    }\n\n    function test_TransferOwnershipOnlyOwner() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.NotOwner.selector);\n        jar.transferOwnership(bob);\n    }\n\n    function testFuzz_MultipleTipsAccumulate(uint96 a, uint96 b) public {\n        vm.assume(a > 0 && b > 0);\n        usdc.mint(alice, uint256(a) + uint256(b));\n\n        _tip(alice, a, \"a\");\n        _tip(alice, b, \"b\");\n\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), uint256(a) + uint256(b));\n        assertEq(jar.tipCount(), 2);\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":52,"oldLines":9,"newStart":52,"newLines":12,"lines":["     }"," ","     function test_TipTransfersAndRecords() public {","+        vm.startPrank(alice);","+        usdc.approve(address(jar), 5 * ONE_USDC);","         vm.expectEmit(true, true, false, true, address(jar));","         emit Tipped(0, alice, 5 * ONE_USDC, \"gm\", block.timestamp);","-        _tip(alice, 5 * ONE_USDC, \"gm\");","+        jar.tip(5 * ONE_USDC, \"gm\");","+        vm.stopPrank();"," ","         assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(address(jar)), 5 * ONE_USDC);","         assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(alice), 95 * ONE_USDC);"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeD3xnHm1e5u9SQwFG7Jc","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_018W4BehKwWBVcLEZLJ3E1Pa","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-20T002040Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/scripts/sync-frontend.mjs","content":"#!/usr/bin/env node\n// Generates frontend/src/generated/contracts.ts from the Foundry build output\n// and the local deployment addresses. Run after `forge build` + deploy.\nimport { readFileSync, writeFileSync, mkdirSync, existsSync } from \"node:fs\";\nimport { dirname, resolve } from \"node:path\";\nimport { fileURLToPath } from \"node:url\";\n\nconst root = resolve(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), \"..\");\nconst contractsDir = resolve(root, \"contracts\");\nconst outDir = resolve(root, \"frontend/src/generated\");\n\nfunction readAbi(name) {\n  const path = resolve(contractsDir, `out/${name}.sol/${name}.json`);\n  if (!existsSync(path)) {\n    throw new Error(`Missing artifact ${path}. Run 'forge build' in contracts/ first.`);\n  }\n  return JSON.parse(readFileSync(path, \"utf8\")).abi;\n}\n\nconst deploymentPath = resolve(contractsDir, \"deployments/local.json\");\nif (!existsSync(deploymentPath)) {\n  throw new Error(\n    `Missing ${deploymentPath}. Deploy first:\\n` +\n      `  cd contracts && PRIVATE_KEY=<anvil key> forge script script/Deploy.s.sol:Deploy --rpc-url http://127.0.0.1:8545 --broadcast`\n  );\n}\nconst deployment = JSON.parse(readFileSync(deploymentPath, \"utf8\"));\n\nconst tipJarAbi = readAbi(\"TipJar\");\nconst usdcAbi = readAbi(\"MockUSDC\");\n\nconst banner = \"// AUTO-GENERATED by scripts/sync-frontend.mjs — do not edit by hand.\";\n\nconst contents = `${banner}\nexport const CHAIN_ID = ${deployment.chainId} as const;\n\nexport const TIP_JAR_ADDRESS = \"${deployment.tipJar}\" as const;\nexport const USDC_ADDRESS = \"${deployment.usdc}\" as const;\n\nexport const tipJarAbi = ${JSON.stringify(tipJarAbi, null, 2)} as const;\n\nexport const usdcAbi = ${JSON.stringify(usdcAbi, null, 2)} as const;\n`;\n\nmkdirSync(outDir, { recursive: true });\nconst target = resolve(outDir, \"contracts.ts\");\nwriteFileSync(target, contents);\nconsole.log(`Wrote ${target}`);\nconsole.log(`  chainId : ${deployment.chainId}`);\nconsole.log(`  TipJar  : ${deployment.tipJar}`);\nconsole.log(`  USDC    : ${deployment.usdc}`);\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1768,"cache_read_input_tokens":39192,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1768},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1f29d859-7c73-4144-867e-a953c615fdc4","uuid":"8786c0e5-f5a0-4afe-aea4-6188a9f38026","timestamp":"2026-08-20T00:27:10.306Z","request_id":"req_011CeD3xmMCkiWg3Z4hgwrBE"}

Run after `forge build` + deploy.\nimport { readFileSync, writeFileSync, mkdirSync, existsSync } from \"node:fs\";\nimport { dirname, resolve } from \"node:path\";\nimport { fileURLToPath } from \"node:url\";\n\nconst root = resolve(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), \"..\");\nconst contractsDir = resolve(root, \"contracts\");\nconst outDir = resolve(root, \"frontend/src/generated\");\n\nfunction readAbi(name) {\n  const path = resolve(contractsDir, `out/${name}.sol/${name}.json`);\n  if (!existsSync(path)) {\n    throw new Error(`Missing artifact ${path}. Run 'forge build' in contracts/ first.`);\n  }\n  return JSON.parse(readFileSync(path, \"utf8\")).abi;\n}\n\nconst deploymentPath = resolve(contractsDir, \"deployments/local.json\");\nif (!existsSync(deploymentPath)) {\n  throw new Error(\n    `Missing ${deploymentPath}. Deploy first:\\n` +\n      `  cd contracts && PRIVATE_KEY=<anvil key> forge script script/Deploy.s.sol:Deploy --rpc-url http://127.0.0.1:8545 --broadcast`\n  );\n}\nconst deployment = JSON.parse(readFileSync(deploymentPath, \"utf8\"));\n\nconst tipJarAbi = readAbi(\"TipJar\");\nconst usdcAbi = readAbi(\"MockUSDC\");\n\nconst banner = \"// AUTO-GENERATED by scripts/sync-frontend.mjs — do not edit by hand.\";\n\nconst contents = `${banner}\nexport const CHAIN_ID = ${deployment.chainId} as const;\n\nexport const TIP_JAR_ADDRESS = \"${deployment.tipJar}\" as const;\nexport const USDC_ADDRESS = \"${deployment.usdc}\" as const;\n\nexport const tipJarAbi = ${JSON.stringify(tipJarAbi, null, 2)} as const;\n\nexport const usdcAbi = ${JSON.stringify(usdcAbi, null, 2)} as const;\n`;\n\nmkdirSync(outDir, { recursive: true });\nconst target = resolve(outDir, \"contracts.ts\");\nwriteFileSync(target, contents);\nconsole.log(`Wrote ${target}`);\nconsole.log(`  chainId : ${deployment.chainId}`);\nconsole.log(`  TipJar  : ${deployment.tipJar}`);\nconsole.log(`  USDC    : ${deployment.usdc}`);\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeD41bRVaRRn21GAxcsux","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Now the wagmi config and React app. First the wagmi setup:"}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":527,"cache_read_input_tokens":44379,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":527},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1f29d859-7c73-4144-867e-a953c615fdc4","uuid":"5b46ca8d-1f16-49e9-9b4c-3fcc4c52dfbc","timestamp":"2026-08-20T00:27:40.657Z","request_id":"req_011CeD41aatMSAyUpA3DFv7o"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeD41bRVaRRn21GAxcsux","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01R3ufx7ZFbNDVvRaSKQjHMs","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-20T002040Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/frontend/src/wagmi.ts","content":"import { http, createConfig } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { foundry } from \"wagmi/chains\";\nimport { injected } from \"wagmi/connectors\";\nimport { CHAIN_ID } from \"./generated/contracts\";\n\n// This project is local-only: the TipJar + MockUSDC live on a local anvil node\n// (chain id 31337, exposed by viem/wagmi as the `foundry` chain). If you ever\n// point it at real Base USDC, add the `base` chain here and redeploy the jar\n// with the canonical USDC address.\nif (foundry.id !== CHAIN_ID) {\n  console.warn(\n    `Deployment chainId (${CHAIN_ID}) does not match the foundry chain (${foundry.id}). ` +\n      \"Re-run the sync script after deploying to the expected network.\"\n  );\n}\n\nexport const config = createConfig({\n  chains: [foundry],\n  connectors: [injected()],\n  transports: {\n    [foundry.id]: http(\"http://127.0.0.1:8545\"),\n  },\n});\n\ndeclare module \"wagmi\" {\n  interface Register {\n    config: typeof config;\n  }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":527,"cache_read_input_tokens":44379,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":527},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1f29d859-7c73-4144-867e-a953c615fdc4","uuid":"7741f1dd-6daa-484b-8692-39306103b767","timestamp":"2026-08-20T00:27:45.620Z","request_id":"req_011CeD41aatMSAyUpA3DFv7o"}
{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01R3ufx7ZFbNDVvRaSKQjHMs","type":"tool_result","content":"File created successfully at: /ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-20T002040Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/frontend/src/wagmi.ts (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1f29d859-7c73-4144-867e-a953c615fdc4","uuid":"b700c6c4-7e22-402c-a1ff-d2580548a760","timestamp":"2026-08-20T00:27:45.626Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"create","filePath":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-20T002040Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/frontend/src/wagmi.ts","content":"import { http, createConfig } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { foundry } from \"wagmi/chains\";\nimport { injected } from \"wagmi/connectors\";\nimport { CHAIN_ID } from \"./generated/contracts\";\n\n// This project is local-only: the TipJar + MockUSDC live on a local anvil node\n// (chain id 31337, exposed by viem/wagmi as the `foundry` chain). If you ever\n// point it at real Base USDC, add the `base` chain here and redeploy the jar\n// with the canonical USDC address.\nif (foundry.id !== CHAIN_ID) {\n  console.warn(\n    `Deployment chainId (${CHAIN_ID}) does not match the foundry chain (${foundry.id}). ` +\n      \"Re-run the sync script after deploying to the expected network.\"\n  );\n}\n\nexport const config = createConfig({\n  chains: [foundry],\n  connectors: [injected()],\n  transports: {\n    [foundry.id]: http(\"http://127.0.0.1:8545\"),\n  },\n});\n\ndeclare module \"wagmi\" {\n  interface Register {\n    config: typeof config;\n  }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeD42ogxStbRwTZRV3pSj","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01BtouLdxA2di85WRyhBoE9h","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-20T002040Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/frontend/src/components/ConnectWallet.tsx","content":"import { useAccount, useConnect, useDisconnect, useSwitchChain } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { foundry } from \"wagmi/chains\";\nimport { shortAddress } from \"../lib/format\";\n\nexport function ConnectWallet() {\n  const { address, isConnected, chainId } = useAccount();\n  const { connect, connectors, isPending, error } = useConnect();\n  const { disconnect } = useDisconnect();\n  const { switchChain } = useSwitchChain();\n\n  if (isConnected && address) {\n    const wrongChain = chainId !== foundry.id;\n    return (\n      <div className=\"wallet\">\n        {wrongChain && (\n          <button className=\"btn btn-warn\" onClick={() => switchChain({ chainId: foundry.id })}>\n            Switch to Localhost\n          </button>\n        )}\n        <span className=\"wallet-address\" title={address}>\n          {shortAddress(address)}\n        </span>\n        <button className=\"btn btn-ghost\" onClick={() => disconnect()}>\n          Disconnect\n        </button>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  const injectedConnector = connectors.find((c) => c.type === \"injected\") ?? connectors[0];\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"wallet\">\n      <button\n        className=\"btn btn-primary\"\n        disabled={!injectedConnector || isPending}\n        onClick={() => injectedConnector && connect({ connector: injectedConnector })}\n      >\n        {isPending ? {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeD441Zs5UFrxJiVHHG2g","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_016mVaPThwtyuUPfinf55xhs","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-20T002040Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/frontend/src/components/TipForm.tsx","content":"import { useState } from \"react\";\nimport { parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport {\n  useAccount,\n  useReadContract,\n  useWaitForTransactionReceipt,\n  useWriteContract,\n} from \"wagmi\";\nimport { foundry } from \"wagmi/chains\";\nimport {\n  TIP_JAR_ADDRESS,\n  USDC_ADDRESS,\n  tipJarAbi,\n  usdcAbi,\n} from \"../generated/contracts\";\nimport { formatUsdc, USDC_DECIMALS } from \"../lib/format\";\n\nexport function TipForm({ onTipped }: { onTipped: () => void }) {\n  const { address, isConnected, chainId } = useAccount();\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [message, setMessage] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const onWrongChain = isConnected && chainId !== foundry.id;\n\n  const balance = useReadContract({\n    address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n    abi: usdcAbi,\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: address ? [address] : undefined,\n    query: { enabled: Boolean(address) },\n  });\n\n  const allowance = useReadContract({\n    address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n    abi: usdcAbi,\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: address ? [address, TIP_JAR_ADDRESS] : undefined,\n    query: { enabled: Boolean(address) },\n  });\n\n  const { writeContractAsync, isPending } = useWriteContract();\n  const [txHash, setTxHash] = useState<`0x${string}` | undefined>();\n  const receipt = useWaitForTransactionReceipt({ hash: txHash });\n\n  const busy = isPending || receipt.isLoading;\n\n  // Parse the typed amount into raw 6-decimal units, guarding bad input.\n  let parsedAmount: bigint | null = null;\n  try {\n    parsedAmount = amount.trim() ? parseUnits(amount.trim(), USDC_DECIMALS) : null;\n  } catch {\n    parsedAmount = null;\n  }\n\n  const currentAllowance = (allowance.data as bigint | undefined) ?? 0n;\n  const currentBalance = (balance.data as bigint | undefined) ?? 0n;\n  const needsApproval = parsedAmount !== null && currentAllowance < parsedAmount;\n  const insufficientBalance = parsedAmount !== null && parsedAmount > currentBalance;\n\n  async function refreshBalances() {\n    await Promise.all([balance.refetch(), allowance.refetch()]);\n  }\n\n  async function handleFaucet() {\n    try {\n      const hash = await writeContractAsync({\n        address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n        abi: usdcAbi,\n        functionName: \"faucet\",\n      });\n      setTxHash(hash);\n      await refreshBalances();\n    } catch (err) {\n      console.error(err);\n    }\n  }\n\n  async function handleApprove() {\n    if (parsedAmount === null) return;\n    try {\n      const hash = await writeContractAsync({\n        address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n        abi: usdcAbi,\n        functionName: \"approve\",\n        args: [TIP_JAR_ADDRESS, parsedAmount],\n      });\n      setTxHash(hash);\n      await allowance.refetch();\n    } catch (err) {\n      console.error(err);\n    }\n  }\n\n  async function handleTip() {\n    if (parsedAmount === null) return;\n    try {\n      const hash = await writeContractAsync({\n        address: TIP_JAR_ADDRESS,\n        abi: tipJarAbi,\n        functionName: \"tip\",\n        args: [parsedAmount, message],\n      });\n      setTxHash(hash);\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      setMessage(\"\");\n      await refreshBalances();\n      onTipped();\n    } catch (err) {\n      console.error(err);\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (!isConnected) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"card\">\n        <h2>Send a tip</h2>\n        <p className=\"muted\">Connect your wallet to leave a tip.</p>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card\">\n      <h2>Send a tip</h2>\n\n      {onWrongChain ? (\n        <p className=\"error-text\">Switch your wallet to the Localhost network to tip.</p>\n      ) : (\n        <>\n          <div className=\"balance-row\">\n            <span className=\"muted\">Your USDC balance</span>\n            <span className=\"balance-value\">{formatUsdc(currentBalance)} USDC</span>\n            <button className=\"btn btn-ghost btn-sm\" onClick={handleFaucet} disabled={busy}>\n              Get 1,000 (faucet)\n            </button>\n          </div>\n\n          <label className=\"field\">\n            <span>Amount (USDC)</span>\n            <input\n              type=\"number\"\n              min=\"0\"\n              step=\"0.01\"\n              placeholder=\"5.00\"\n              value={amount}\n              onChange={(e) => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n            />\n          </label>\n\n          <label className=\"field\">\n            <span>Message (optional)</span>\n            <input\n              type=\"text\"\n              maxLength={140}\n              placeholder=\"gm ☕\"\n              value={message}\n              onChange={(e) => setMessage(e.target.value)}\n            />\n          </label>\n\n          {insufficientBalance && (\n            <p className=\"error-text\">Not enough USDC — use the faucet above.</p>\n          )}\n\n          {needsApproval ? (\n            <button\n              className=\"btn btn-primary\"\n              onClick={handleApprove}\n              disabled={busy || parsedAmount === null || insufficientBalance}\n            >\n              {busy ? \"Approving…\" : \"Approve USDC\"}\n            </button>\n          ) : (\n            <button\n              className=\"btn btn-primary\"\n              onClick={handleTip}\n              disabled={busy || parsedAmount === null || insufficientBalance}\n            >\n              {busy ? \"Sending…\" : \"Send tip\"}\n            </button>\n          )}\n\n          <p className=\"hint muted\">\n            Tipping is two steps the first time: approve the jar to move your USDC, then send.\n          </p>\n        </>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":740,"cache_read_input_tokens":47384,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":740},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1f29d859-7c73-4144-867e-a953c615fdc4","uuid":"ee31e294-1c0b-4680-9789-f109abb7c5ea","timestamp":"2026-08-20T00:28:33.212Z","request_id":"req_011CeD43zMgSJ5bxPjCyBZVd"}

aSQy+uexXpgGqoiKkKi6QS5+s4Qz00uIOAZH1ld91jb86GMpGnZ1d0WcwemGhlhRpyyw3uyPBC1oTSj8bN5UtusPvWGAE="}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2746,"cache_read_input_tokens":48124,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2746},"output_tokens":7,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1f29d859-7c73-4144-867e-a953c615fdc4","uuid":"52d3a7f1-0348-4546-b3c7-4d76166c73aa","timestamp":"2026-08-20T00:28:42.720Z","request_id":"req_011CeD45yHkMdB4o4XLtd3TH"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeD464dDgtR6Z2fa5zWYK","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Now the tip feed:"}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2746,"cache_read_input_tokens":48124,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2746},"output_tokens":7,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1f29d859-7c73-4144-867e-a953c615fdc4","uuid":"0bf886e5-c929-400d-985f-a6d45c98b05a","timestamp":"2026-08-20T00:28:42.721Z","request_id":"req_011CeD45yHkMdB4o4XLtd3TH"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeD464dDgtR6Z2fa5zWYK","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Loj3v73SjXNfAWrVaar3zj","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-20T002040Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/frontend/src/components/TipFeed.tsx","content":"import { useEffect } from \"react\";\nimport { useReadContract, useWatchContractEvent } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { TIP_JAR_ADDRESS, tipJarAbi } from \"../generated/contracts\";\nimport { formatUsdc, shortAddress, timeAgo } from \"../lib/format\";\n\ntype Tip = {\n  from: `0x${string}`;\n  amount: bigint;\n  timestamp: bigint;\n  message: string;\n};\n\nexport function TipFeed({ refreshSignal }: { refreshSignal: number }) {\n  const tips = useReadContract({\n    address: TIP_JAR_ADDRESS,\n    abi: tipJarAbi,\n    functionName: \"getLatestTips\",\n    args: [25n],\n  });\n\n  const totalTipped = useReadContract({\n    address: TIP_JAR_ADDRESS,\n    abi: tipJarAbi,\n    functionName: \"totalTipped\",\n  });\n\n  const tipCount = useReadContract({\n    address: TIP_JAR_ADDRESS,\n    abi: tipJarAbi,\n    functionName: \"tipCount\",\n  });\n\n  function refetchAll() {\n    tips.refetch();\n    totalTipped.refetch();\n    tipCount.refetch();\n  }\n\n  // Refresh when this tab sends a tip.\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (refreshSignal > 0) refetchAll();\n    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\n  }, [refreshSignal]);\n\n  // Refresh when anyone tips (picked up by polling the Tipped event).\n  useWatchContractEvent({\n    address: TIP_JAR_ADDRESS,\n    abi: tipJarAbi,\n    eventName: \"Tipped\",\n    onLogs: refetchAll,\n  });\n\n  const list = (tips.data as readonly Tip[] | undefined) ?? [];\n  const total = (totalTipped.data as bigint | undefined) ?? 0n;\n  const count = (tipCount.data as bigint | undefined) ?? 0n;\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card\">\n      <div className=\"feed-header\">\n        <h2>Tip feed</h2>\n        <div className=\"stats\">\n          <div className=\"stat\">\n            <span className=\"stat-value\">{formatUsdc(total)}</span>\n            <span className=\"stat-label\">USDC tipped</span>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"stat\">\n            <span className=\"stat-value\">{count.toString()}</span>\n            <span className=\"stat-label\">tips</span>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      {tips.isLoading && <p className=\"muted\">Loading tips…</p>}\n      {tips.isError && (\n        <p className=\"error-text\">\n          Couldn't read the jar. Is anvil running and the contract deployed?\n        </p>\n      )}\n\n      {!tips.isLoading && list.length === 0 && (\n        <p className=\"muted\">No tips yet — be the first!</p>\n      )}\n\n      <ul className=\"feed\">\n        {list.map((tip, i) => (\n          <li className=\"feed-item\" key={`${tip.timestamp}-${i}`}>\n            <div className=\"feed-item-top\">\n              <span className=\"feed-from\" title={tip.from}>\n                {shortAddress(tip.from)}\n              </span>\n              <span className=\"feed-amount\">+{formatUsdc(tip.amount)} USDC</span>\n            </div>\n            {tip.message && <p className=\"feed-message\">“{tip.message}”</p>}\n            <span className=\"feed-time muted\">{timeAgo(tip.timestamp)}</span>\n          </li>\n        ))}\n      </ul>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2746,"cache_read_input_tokens":48124,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2746},"output_tokens":7,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1f29d859-7c73-4144-867e-a953c615fdc4","uuid":"f8999300-9350-42bc-8ce2-ca02f430ebac","timestamp":"2026-08-20T00:28:55.044Z","request_id":"req_011CeD45yHkMdB4o4XLtd3TH"}

cursor: pointer;\n  transition: background 0.15s, opacity 0.15s;\n}\n\n.btn:disabled {\n  opacity: 0.5;\n  cursor: not-allowed;\n}\n\n.btn-primary {\n  width: 100%;\n  background: var(--accent);\n  color: white;\n}\n\n.btn-primary:hover:not(:disabled) {\n  background: var(--accent-2);\n}\n\n.btn-ghost {\n  background: transparent;\n  border-color: var(--border);\n  color: 12px;\n}\n\n.wallet {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 10px;\n  flex-wrap: wrap;\n}\n\n.wallet-address {\n  font-family: ui-monospace, monospace;\n  background: var(--panel-2);\n
